Theorem 3.1 thm:comparison
Disjoint P,N on F U; partial s and ℓ with reversed fibers and Fℓ = s ≫ χ.hom; ∃ total g : U ⟶ U with both inverse images zero, and specified-join noncoverage.

theorem comparison_zero_inverse_images (F : RangeFunctor R A) {U : R}
    (τ : nearProd U U ⟶ U) (hτ : RecursionCategory.IsTuringMorphism τ)
    (b : U ≅ coprod U U) (P N : Idem (F.toFunctor.obj U))
    (hPN : P ⊓ N = ⊥) (ha0 : F.mapIdem (diagonal0 τ b) ≤ P)
    (ha1 : F.mapIdem (diagonal1 τ b) ≤ N)
    (s : F.toFunctor.obj U ⟶ coprod (F.toFunctor.obj U) (F.toFunctor.obj U))

    (hs1 : Idem.pull s (summand1 (F.toFunctor.obj U) (F.toFunctor.obj U)) = P)
    (ℓ : U ⟶ coprod U U)
    (hℓ : F.toFunctor.map ℓ = s ≫ (F.comparison U U).hom) :
    ∃ g : U ⟶ U, isTotal g ∧
      Idem.pull (F.toFunctor.map g) P = ⊥ ∧
      Idem.pull (F.toFunctor.map g) N = ⊥ := by

Corollary 3.2 cor:separator
No partial conditional s : F U ⟶ F U+F U and partial source lift ℓ exist for a complementary target separator.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.LocalConnectedness.no_lifted_complementary_separator Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46Comparison.lean:112
theorem no_lifted_complementary_separator [BinaryJoins A] [TotalJoinInverse A]
    (F : RangeFunctor R A) {U : R}
    (τ : nearProd U U ⟶ U) (hτ : RecursionCategory.IsTuringMorphism τ)
    (b : U ≅ coprod U U) (hU : F.toFunctor.map (𝟙 U) ≠ 0)
    (P N : Idem (F.toFunctor.obj U)) (hPN : Complementary P N)
    (ha0 : F.mapIdem (diagonal0 τ b) ≤ P)
    (ha1 : F.mapIdem (diagonal1 τ b) ≤ N) :
    ¬ ∃ (s : F.toFunctor.obj U ⟶ coprod (F.toFunctor.obj U) (F.toFunctor.obj U))
      (ℓ : U ⟶ coprod U U),
      Idem.pull s (summand0 (F.toFunctor.obj U) (F.toFunctor.obj U)) = N ∧
      Idem.pull s (summand1 (F.toFunctor.obj U) (F.toFunctor.obj U)) = P ∧
      F.toFunctor.map ℓ = s ≫ (F.comparison U U).hom := by

Corollary 3.3 cor:source-conditionals
Source diagonal0 τ b and diagonal1 τ b are inseparable when every complementary source pair admits the specified total conditional.

    (F : RangeFunctor R A) {U : R}

    (b : U ≅ coprod U U) (hU : F.toFunctor.map (𝟙 U) ≠ 0)
    (conditionals : ∀ c c' : Idem U, Complementary c c' →
      ∃ ℓ : U ⟶ coprod U U, isTotal ℓ ∧
        c.hom ≫ ℓ = c.hom ≫ i1 U U ∧ c'.hom ≫ ℓ = c'.hom ≫ i0 U U) :

Proposition 3.4 prop:disjoint-joins
The disjoint join of c ≫ i1 and c′ ≫ i0 is a total conditional X ⟶ X+X.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.LocalConnectedness.complementary_conditional_of_disjoint_joins Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46Comparison.lean:183
theorem complementary_conditional_of_disjoint_joins [DisjointArrowJoins C]
    {X : C} (c c' : Idem X) (hc : PairComplementary c c') :
    ∃ ℓ : X ⟶ coprod X X, isTotal ℓ ∧
      c.hom ≫ ℓ = c.hom ≫ i1 X X ∧ c'.hom ≫ ℓ = c'.hom ≫ i0 X X ∧
      ∀ h : X ⟶ coprod X X,
        ArrowLE (c.hom ≫ i1 X X) h → ArrowLE (c'.hom ≫ i0 X X) h → ArrowLE ℓ h :=
  disjoint_joins_switch c c' hc


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.LocalConnectedness.disjoint_joins_switch Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/DisjointJoins.lean:44
theorem disjoint_joins_switch [DisjointArrowJoins C] {X : C}
    (c c' : Idem X) (hc : PairComplementary c c') :
    ∃ ℓ : X ⟶ coprod X X, isTotal ℓ ∧
      c.hom ≫ ℓ = c.hom ≫ i1 X X ∧ c'.hom ≫ ℓ = c'.hom ≫ i0 X X ∧
      ∀ h : X ⟶ coprod X X,
        ArrowLE (c.hom ≫ i1 X X) h → ArrowLE (c'.hom ≫ i0 X X) h → ArrowLE ℓ h := by

Proposition 4.1 prop:interpreted-theory
Interpretation preserves the range and splitting equations for sentence c : P ⟶ S; a sound factorization through v gives semantic consistency.

theorem interpreted_theory_equations (G : RangeFunctor C A) {P T S : C}
    (c : P ⟶ S) (m : T ⟶ S) (r : S ⟶ T) (t : Idem S)
    (ht : t = Ranges.range c) (hmr : m ≫ r = 𝟙 T) (hrm : r ≫ m = t.hom) :
    G.mapIdem t = Ranges.range (G.toFunctor.map c) ∧
      G.toFunctor.map r ≫ G.toFunctor.map m = (G.mapIdem t).hom ∧
      G.toFunctor.map m ≫ G.toFunctor.map r = 𝟙 (G.toFunctor.obj T) := by

theorem interpreted_theory (G : RangeFunctor C (Par D)) {P T S : C}
    (c : P ⟶ S) (hc : isTotal c) (m : T ⟶ S) (hm : isTotal m)
    (r : S ⟶ T) (t : Idem S) (ht : t = Ranges.range c)
    (hmr : m ≫ r = 𝟙 T) (hrm : r ≫ m = t.hom)
    {V : D} (v : V ⟶ (G.toFunctor.obj S).obj) [Mono v]
    (k : (G.toFunctor.obj P).obj ⟶ V)
    (hfac : k ≫ v = totalValue (G.toFunctor.map c) (G.map_total c hc)) (ν : S ⟶ S) :
    G.mapIdem t = Ranges.range (G.toFunctor.map c) ∧
      G.toFunctor.map r ≫ G.toFunctor.map m = (G.mapIdem t).hom ∧
      G.toFunctor.map m ≫ G.toFunctor.map r = 𝟙 (G.toFunctor.obj T) ∧
      Mono (totalValue (G.toFunctor.map m) (G.map_total m hm)) ∧
      (∃ w : (G.toFunctor.obj T).obj ⟶ V,
        w ≫ v = totalValue (G.toFunctor.map m) (G.map_total m hm)) ∧
      (validityDomain v ⊓ Idem.pull (G.toFunctor.map ν) (validityDomain v) = ⊥ →
        G.mapIdem t ⊓ Idem.pull (G.toFunctor.map ν) (G.mapIdem t) = ⊥) := by

theorem theory_subobject {P T S : Par D}
    (c : P ⟶ S) (hc : isTotal c) (m : T ⟶ S) (hm : isTotal m) (r : S ⟶ T)
    (hmr : m ≫ r = 𝟙 T) (hrm : r ≫ m = (Ranges.range c).hom)
    {V : D} (v : V ⟶ S.obj) [Mono v] (k : P.obj ⟶ V)
    (hfac : k ≫ v = totalValue c hc) :
    Mono (totalValue m hm) ∧ ∃ w : T.obj ⟶ V, w ≫ v = totalValue m hm :=
  ⟨totalValue_mono_of_retraction m hm r hmr,
    theory_subobject_factorization c hc m hm r hmr hrm v k hfac⟩

Theorem 4.2 thm:logical-comparison
For an interpreted sentence theory t and total θ, construct total g with zero theorem/refutation inverse images; deduce interpreted and source noncoverage.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.LocalConnectedness.logical_comparison Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46LogicalComparison.lean:185
theorem logical_comparison (F : RangeFunctor R A)
    (splittings : ∀ X : SplitObject R, V46.TargetSplitting F X) {U : R}
    (τ : nearProd U U ⟶ U) (hτ : RecursionCategory.IsTuringMorphism τ)
    (b : U ≅ coprod U U) (hU : F.toFunctor.map (𝟙 U) ≠ 0)
    {S : SplitObject R} (θ : (splittingInclusion (C := R)).obj U ⟶ S)
    (hθ : isTotal θ) (ν : S ⟶ S) (tA : Idem S)
    (ht : (V46.chosenSplitRangeFunctor F splittings).mapIdem tA ⊓
      Idem.pull ((V46.chosenSplitRangeFunctor F splittings).toFunctor.map ν)
        ((V46.chosenSplitRangeFunctor F splittings).mapIdem tA) = ⊥)
    (ha0 : F.mapIdem (diagonal0 τ b) ≤
      Idem.pull ((V46.chosenSplitExtensionInclusionIso F splittings).inv.app U ≫
        (V46.chosenSplitExtension F splittings).map θ)
        ((V46.chosenSplitRangeFunctor F splittings).mapIdem tA))
    (ha1 : F.mapIdem (diagonal1 τ b) ≤
      Idem.pull ((V46.chosenSplitExtensionInclusionIso F splittings).inv.app U ≫
        (V46.chosenSplitExtension F splittings).map θ)
        (Idem.pull ((V46.chosenSplitExtension F splittings).map ν)
          ((V46.chosenSplitRangeFunctor F splittings).mapIdem tA)))
    (s : F.toFunctor.obj U ⟶ coprod (F.toFunctor.obj U) (F.toFunctor.obj U))
    (hs0 : Idem.pull s (summand0 (F.toFunctor.obj U) (F.toFunctor.obj U)) =
      Idem.pull ((V46.chosenSplitExtensionInclusionIso F splittings).inv.app U ≫
        (V46.chosenSplitExtension F splittings).map θ)
        (Idem.pull ((V46.chosenSplitExtension F splittings).map ν)
          ((V46.chosenSplitRangeFunctor F splittings).mapIdem tA)))
    (hs1 : Idem.pull s (summand1 (F.toFunctor.obj U) (F.toFunctor.obj U)) =
      Idem.pull ((V46.chosenSplitExtensionInclusionIso F splittings).inv.app U ≫
        (V46.chosenSplitExtension F splittings).map θ)
        ((V46.chosenSplitRangeFunctor F splittings).mapIdem tA))
    (ℓ : U ⟶ coprod U U)
    (hℓ : F.toFunctor.map ℓ = s ≫ (F.comparison U U).hom) :
    ∃ g : U ⟶ U, isTotal g ∧
      Idem.pull ((V46.chosenSplitExtension F splittings).map
        ((splittingInclusion (C := R)).map g ≫ θ))
        ((V46.chosenSplitRangeFunctor F splittings).mapIdem tA) = ⊥ ∧
      Idem.pull ((V46.chosenSplitExtension F splittings).map
        ((splittingInclusion (C := R)).map g ≫ θ))
        (Idem.pull ((V46.chosenSplitExtension F splittings).map ν)
          ((V46.chosenSplitRangeFunctor F splittings).mapIdem tA)) = ⊥ ∧
      (V46.chosenSplitRangeFunctor F splittings).mapIdem tA ⊔
        Idem.pull ((V46.chosenSplitExtension F splittings).map ν)
          ((V46.chosenSplitRangeFunctor F splittings).mapIdem tA) ≠ ⊤ ∧
      tA ⊔ Idem.pull ν tA ≠ ⊤ := by

theorem logical_comparison_target (F : RangeFunctor R A) {U : R}
    (τ : nearProd U U ⟶ U) (hτ : RecursionCategory.IsTuringMorphism τ)
    (b : U ≅ coprod U U) (hU : F.toFunctor.map (𝟙 U) ≠ 0)
    {S : A} (θ : F.toFunctor.obj U ⟶ S) (hθ : isTotal θ)
    (ν : S ⟶ S) (t : Idem S) (ht : t ⊓ Idem.pull ν t = ⊥)
    (ha0 : F.mapIdem (diagonal0 τ b) ≤ Idem.pull θ t)
    (ha1 : F.mapIdem (diagonal1 τ b) ≤ Idem.pull θ (Idem.pull ν t))
    (s : F.toFunctor.obj U ⟶ coprod (F.toFunctor.obj U) (F.toFunctor.obj U))
    (hs0 : Idem.pull s (summand0 (F.toFunctor.obj U) (F.toFunctor.obj U)) =
      Idem.pull θ (Idem.pull ν t))
    (hs1 : Idem.pull s (summand1 (F.toFunctor.obj U) (F.toFunctor.obj U)) =
      Idem.pull θ t)
    (ℓ : U ⟶ coprod U U)
    (hℓ : F.toFunctor.map ℓ = s ≫ (F.comparison U U).hom) :
    ∃ g : U ⟶ U, isTotal g ∧
      Idem.pull (F.toFunctor.map g ≫ θ) t = ⊥ ∧
      Idem.pull (F.toFunctor.map g ≫ θ) (Idem.pull ν t) = ⊥ ∧
      t ⊔ Idem.pull ν t ≠ ⊤ := by

Corollary 4.3 cor:domain-incompleteness
Every consistent extension t ≥ t₀ has inseparable theorem/refutation idempotents and t ∨ ν*t ≠ 1.

theorem domain_incompleteness [TotalJoinInverse C] {U S : C}
    {a b : Idem U} (hab : Inseparable a b)
    (θ : U ⟶ S) (hθ : isTotal θ) (ν : S ⟶ S) (_hν : isTotal ν)

    (hb : b ≤ Idem.pull θ (Idem.pull ν t₀))
    (t : Idem S) (ht : t₀ ≤ t) (hconsistent : t ⊓ Idem.pull ν t = ⊥) :
    Inseparable t (Idem.pull ν t) ∧ t ⊔ Idem.pull ν t ≠ ⊤ := by

def admissibleInterpretation : S.admissibleSubstitution ⟶ Q.admissibleSubstitution :=
  pullback.lift
    (S.admissibleInclusion ≫
      prod.map (interpretation S Q f).2 (substitutionParameterMap S Q f))
    (S.formulaSubstitution ≫ (interpretation S Q f).2) (by
      rw [Category.assoc, ← formula_substitution_option S Q f,
        ← Category.assoc, S.formulaSubstitution_square, Category.assoc, OptionSyntax.map_ok]
      simp only [Category.assoc])

    admissibleInterpretation S Q f ≫ Q.admissibleInclusion =
      S.admissibleInclusion ≫
        prod.map (interpretation S Q f).2 (substitutionParameterMap S Q f) :=
  pullback.lift_fst _ _ _

Corollary 4.8 cor:signed-first-order-incompleteness
For a disjoint pair with consistent free signed theory, that theory is essentially incomplete exactly when the pair is inseparable in the restriction category.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.SignedFirstOrder.essential_incomplete_iff_inseparable Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46SignedFirstOrderIncompleteness.lean:29
theorem essential_incomplete_iff_inseparable
    (inverses : RestrictionSubobjects.MonoInverses C)
    {V X : RestrictionSubobjects.Tot C} (F : NullaryPresentations V)
    (a b : Subobject X) (hab : a ⊓ b = ⊥)
    (consistent : (F.presentation X).Consistent (theory F a b).val) :
    (F.presentation X).EssentiallyIncomplete (theory F a b) ↔
      Inseparable (RestrictionSubobjects.domain inverses a)
        (RestrictionSubobjects.domain inverses b) :=
  (essential_incomplete_iff_subobject_inseparable F a b hab consistent).trans
    (RestrictionSubobjects.inseparable_iff inverses a b).symm

Theorem 5.2 thm:theory-extension
For t=ui, Σ_i is fully faithful and Σ_i⊣i*⊣Σ_i. Proper i gives failure of essential surjectivity; composition and the sentence/theorem comparison square hold. The sentence family is isomorphic to its pullback and the theorem family lies below the pulled-back theory.

Theorem 5.3 thm:associated-incompleteness
Essential incompleteness of u is equivalent to that of B_U(u). Incompleteness is equivalent to non-totality of the associated Boolean partial morphism; inseparability in C is equivalent to no total extension in C[U]. For every consistent closed extension u≥t of a theory receiving the printed total reduction of an inseparable pair, B_U(u) is essentially incomplete and the associated Boolean partial morphism has no total extension.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.AssociatedCategory.associated_incompleteness Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46AssociatedIncompleteness.lean:57
theorem associated_incompleteness (U : P.Theories) (hcU : P.Consistent U.val) :
    (P.EssentiallyIncomplete U ↔
      AssociatedClosedTheories.EssentiallyIncomplete P (U.val : Tot C) (Q (U.val : Tot C))
        (constantTheory P (U.val : Tot C) (Q (U.val : Tot C)) (hQ (U.val : Tot C)) U)) ∧
    (¬ P.Complete U.val ↔
      ¬ isTotal ((PartialSliceTheoryFamilies.constantFunctor inverses (U.val : Tot C)).map
        (theoryDecision inverses P U.val hcU))) ∧

Lemma 6.2 lem:syntactic-functional-search
Every Σ₁ Γ has an index e with PA-provable graph soundness and exact existential domain; T-provable functionality gives T-provable uniform graph equivalence.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.SyntacticSearch.functional_search Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46SyntacticSearch.lean:114
theorem functional_search (F : Graph) :
    ∃ e : ℕ, (𝗣𝗔 ⊢ soundSentence e F) ∧ (𝗣𝗔 ⊢ domainSentence e F) ∧
      ∀ (T : ArithmeticTheory) [𝗣𝗔 ⪯ T],
        (T ⊢ functionalSentence F) → Uniform T (eventualGraph e) F :=
  ⟨searchIndex F, search_sound F, search_domain F,
    fun T _ hF => search_uniform_of_functional T F hF⟩

Proposition 6.5 prop:st-syntax-initiality
Initial K-algebra on (Tm,Fm), for arbitrary coefficient objects, remains initial in every parameter slice.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.SyntacticSyntax.foldHom Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46SyntacticSyntaxFoldHom.lean:148
def foldHom : (SyntaxPolynomial.algebraWithParameters a b (postfixAlgebra T a b) P) ⟶ B where

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.SyntacticSyntax.foldHom_unique Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46SyntacticSyntaxUnique.lean:48
theorem foldHom_unique : H = foldHom T a b B := by

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.SyntacticSyntax.withParametersInitial Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46SyntacticSyntaxInitial.lean:28
def withParametersInitial (P : PredicateCategory T)
    (a b : SyntaxPolynomial (PredicateCategory T)) :
    IsInitial (SyntaxPolynomial.algebraWithParameters a b (postfixAlgebra T a b) P) :=
  IsInitial.ofUniqueHom (fun B => foldHom T a b B)
    (fun B H => foldHom_unique T a b B H)


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.SyntacticSyntax.postfixInitial Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46SyntacticSyntaxInitial.lean:34
def postfixInitial (a b : SyntaxPolynomial (PredicateCategory T)) :
    IsInitial (postfixAlgebra T a b) :=
  initialOfTerminalParameters a b (postfixAlgebra T a b)
    (withParametersInitial T (⊤_ (PredicateCategory T)) a b)


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.SyntacticSyntax.categoricalSyntax Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46SyntacticSyntaxInitial.lean:41

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.SyntacticLists.withParametersInitial Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46SyntacticListsInitial.lean:54
def withParametersInitial : IsInitial ((algebra T V).withParameters P).asAlgebra :=
  IsInitial.ofUniqueHom (foldHom T P V) (by
    intro B m
    apply Endofunctor.Algebra.Hom.ext
    apply fold_unique T (targetListAlgebra T P V B) m.f
    · have h := congrArg (fun k => coprod.inl ≫ k) m.h
      change coprod.inl ≫
        (coprod.map (𝟙 (⊤_ (Over P))) (prod.map (𝟙 (constantFamily P V)) m.f) ≫ B.str) =
          coprod.inl ≫ (coprod.desc ((algebra T V).withParameters P).nil
            ((algebra T V).withParameters P).cons ≫ m.f) at h
      rw [← Category.assoc, coprod.inl_map, Category.id_comp,
        ← Category.assoc, coprod.inl_desc] at h
      exact h.symm
    · have h := congrArg (fun k => coprod.inr ≫ k) m.h
      change coprod.inr ≫
        (coprod.map (𝟙 (⊤_ (Over P))) (prod.map (𝟙 (constantFamily P V)) m.f) ≫ B.str) =
          coprod.inr ≫ (coprod.desc ((algebra T V).withParameters P).nil
            ((algebra T V).withParameters P).cons ≫ m.f) at h
      simp only [← Category.assoc, coprod.inr_map, coprod.inr_desc] at h
      rw [Category.assoc] at h
      exact h.symm)

Proposition 6.7 prop:st-substitution-realization
The list, variable-occurrence and substitution operations agree with the categorical folds. Admissibility is complemented on the object of supported inputs. Arbitrary signature carriers may be c.e. on numerical codes.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ConcreteNamedComparison.admissible_iff_source_and_success Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46SubstitutionSource.lean:71
theorem admissible_iff_source_and_success (n : M) :
    (plainAdmissibleCarrier T V σ π d).val.Evalb ![n] ↔
      ∃ h : (Y ⨯ P : PredicateCategory T).val.Evalb ![n],
        let q : Obj T M (Y ⨯ P) := ⟨n, h⟩
        substitutionFreeFor V σ π d
          ((F T M).map (prod.fst : P ⟶ V) ((F T M).map prod.snd q)).val
          ((F T M).map (prod.snd : P ⟶ X) ((F T M).map prod.snd q)).val
          ((F T M).map prod.fst q).val = 1 := by

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ConcreteNamedComparison.substitutionBranch_enumerable Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46SubstitutionSource.lean:42
theorem substitutionBranch_enumerable (j : ℕ) :
    REPred (fun p : ℕ × ℕ × ℕ => substitutionSource V σ π d p ∧
      substitutionFreeFor V σ π d p.1 p.2.1 p.2.2 = j) :=
  supportedTag_enumerable (carrier_enumerable V)
    (carrier_enumerable (plainSyntaxCarrier V σ π d false))
    (carrier_enumerable (plainSyntaxCarrier V σ π d true))
    (substitutionFreeFor_primrec V σ π d) j

Theorem 6.9 thm:syntactic-generated-theory
The generated first-order theory is the least closed formula/sentence subobject, represented by a source restriction idempotent.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.SyntacticGeneratedTheory.proofInitial Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46SyntacticGeneratedTheory.lean:38
def proofInitial {A : PredicateCategory T} (axioms : A ⟶ (S).sentenceObject) :
    IsInitial (proofAlgebra T V σ π d axioms) :=
  SyntacticDerivation.derivationInitial T ((S).logicalRules axioms)


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.SyntacticGeneratedTheory.generated_theory Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46SyntacticGeneratedTheory.lean:61
theorem generated_theory {A : PredicateCategory T} (axioms : A ⟶ (S).sentenceObject) :
    ClosedUnder ((S).logicalRules axioms).inference
        (image.ι (conclusion T V σ π d axioms)) ∧
      (∀ {U : PredicateCategory T} (m : U ⟶ (S).algebra.a.2) [Mono m],
        ClosedUnder ((S).logicalRules axioms).inference m →
          ∃ w : image (conclusion T V σ π d axioms) ⟶ U,
            w ≫ m = image.ι (conclusion T V σ π d axioms)) ∧
      (∃ a : (S).logicalParameters ⨿ A ⟶ image (conclusion T V σ π d axioms),
        a ≫ image.ι (conclusion T V σ π d axioms) =
          coprod.desc (S).logicalAxioms (axioms ≫ (S).sentenceInclusion)) ∧
      sentenceTheory T V σ π d axioms =
        (Subobject.pullback (S).sentenceInclusion).obj (formulaTheory T V σ π d axioms) :=
  (S).generated_firstOrder_theory axioms (proofAlgebra T V σ π d axioms)
    (proofInitial T V σ π d axioms)


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.SyntacticGeneratedTheory.sentence_theory_pullback Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46SyntacticGeneratedTheory.lean:76
theorem sentence_theory_pullback {A : PredicateCategory T} (axioms : A ⟶ (S).sentenceObject) :
    sentenceTheory T V σ π d axioms =
      (Subobject.pullback (S).sentenceInclusion).obj (formulaTheory T V σ π d axioms) :=
  (generated_theory T V σ π d axioms).2.2.2

Lemma 7.2 lem:switch
Every complementary pair in S_T has a total conditional; every model sends it to the specified unique section.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.LocalConnectedness.SyntacticModel.syntactic_finite_switch Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/SyntacticFiniteSwitch.lean:213
theorem syntactic_finite_switch {X : CodedProgramCategory T} (c c' : Idem X)
    (hc : Complementary c c') :
    ∃ s : X ⟶ coprod X X, isTotal s ∧
      c.hom ≫ s = c.hom ≫ i1 X X ∧ c'.hom ≫ s = c'.hom ≫ i0 X X := by

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.LocalConnectedness.SyntacticModel.family_finite_switch Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/SyntacticFiniteSwitch.lean:224
theorem family_finite_switch {X : CodedProgramCategory T} (c c' : Idem X)
    (hc : Complementary c c') :
    ∃ s : X ⟶ coprod X X, isTotal s ∧
      c.hom ≫ s = c.hom ≫ i1 X X ∧ c'.hom ≫ s = c'.hom ≫ i0 X X ∧
      (familyFunctor T).map s =
        switchSection ((familyRangeFunctor T).mapIdem c) ((familyRangeFunctor T).mapIdem c')
          ((familyRangeFunctor T).map_complementary hc) ≫
            ((familyRangeFunctor T).comparison X X).hom := by

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.LocalConnectedness.image_switch_eq Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/DisjointJoins.lean:85
theorem image_switch_eq (F : RangeFunctor C A) {X : C}
    (c c' : Idem X) (hc : Complementary c c') (ℓ : X ⟶ coprod X X)
    (h1 : c.hom ≫ ℓ = c.hom ≫ i1 X X)
    (h0 : c'.hom ≫ ℓ = c'.hom ≫ i0 X X) :
    F.toFunctor.map ℓ =
      switchSection (F.mapIdem c) (F.mapIdem c') (F.map_complementary hc) ≫
        (F.comparison X X).hom := by

theorem syntactic_pair_inseparable_v46 (hT : Consistent T)
    (τ : nearProd (omegaObject T) (omegaObject T) ⟶ omegaObject T)
    (hτ : RecursionCategory.IsTuringMorphism τ)
    (b : omegaObject T ≅ coprod (omegaObject T) (omegaObject T)) :
    Inseparable (diagonal0 τ b) (diagonal1 τ b) :=
  source_inseparable_of_complementary_conditionals (familyRangeFunctor T)
    τ hτ b (family_nonzero T hT) (fun c c' hc => syntactic_finite_switch T c c' hc)

    Inseparable (syntacticPair0 T) (syntacticPair1 T) :=
  syntactic_pair_inseparable_v46 T hT (typedTau T) (typedTau_isTuringMorphism T)
    (omegaCoprodIso T).symm


Proposition 7.4 prop:model-total-regular
G_M on the actual total restriction splitting preserves finite limits, finite coproducts and regular images.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.PredicateInterpretation.regular Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46PredicateInterpretationRegular.lean:221
theorem regular : PreservesFiniteLimits (functor T M) ∧
    PreservesFiniteCoproducts (functor T M) ∧
    ∀ (C D : PredicateCategory T) (f : C ⟶ D),
      ∃ e : (functor T M).obj (image f) ≅ image ((functor T M).map f),
        e.hom ≫ image.ι ((functor T M).map f) = (functor T M).map (image.ι f) :=
  ⟨inferInstance, inferInstance, fun _ _ f => preserves_regularImage T M f⟩


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.PredicateInterpretation.total_regular Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46PredicateInterpretationRegular.lean:266
theorem total_regular : PreservesFiniteLimits (totalFunctor T M) ∧
    PreservesFiniteCoproducts (totalFunctor T M) ∧
    ∀ (X Y : TotalSplit T) (f : X ⟶ Y),
      ∃ e : (totalFunctor T M).obj (image f) ≅ image ((totalFunctor T M).map f),
        e.hom ≫ image.ι ((totalFunctor T M).map f) = (totalFunctor T M).map (image.ι f) :=
  ⟨inferInstance, inferInstance,
    fun _ _ f => ⟨totalRegularImageIso T M f, totalRegularImageIso_hom_ι T M f⟩⟩

theorem evaluate_termSubstitution (q : Obj 𝗣𝗔 ℕ (X ⨯ P)) (ρ : ℕ → K) :

      evaluate σ π d functions ((G).map (prod.fst (C := PredicateCategory 𝗣𝗔) («X» := X) («Y» := P)) q)
        (Function.update ρ
          ((G).map (prod.fst (C := PredicateCategory 𝗣𝗔) («X» := (variableCarrier : PredicateCategory 𝗣𝗔)) («Y» := X))
            ((G).map (prod.snd (C := PredicateCategory 𝗣𝗔) («X» := X) («Y» := P)) q)).val
          (evaluate σ π d functions ((G).map (prod.snd (C := PredicateCategory 𝗣𝗔) («X» := (variableCarrier : PredicateCategory 𝗣𝗔)) («Y» := X))
            ((G).map (prod.snd (C := PredicateCategory 𝗣𝗔) («X» := X) («Y» := P)) q)) ρ)) := by

Proposition 7.6 prop:standard-code-soundness
Standard-code derivation conclusions factor uniquely through validity V_K; validity and its negation preimage are disjoint.

theorem valid_logicalAxioms (l : Obj 𝗣𝗔 ℕ (S).logicalParameters) :
    Valid σ π d functions relations ((G).map (S).logicalAxioms l) :=
  logicalAxioms_predicate 𝗣𝗔 ℕ (S) (Valid σ π d functions relations)
    (valid_hilbertOne σ π d functions relations)
    (valid_hilbertTwo σ π d functions relations)
    (valid_hilbertThree σ π d functions relations)
    (valid_hilbertInstantiation σ π d functions relations)
    (valid_hilbertDistribution σ π d functions relations)
    (valid_hilbertReflexivity σ π d functions relations)
    (valid_hilbertEquality σ π d functions relations) l

theorem conclusion_property
    (hlogical : ∀ l : Obj T ℕ S.logicalParameters, Q ((F T ℕ).map S.logicalAxioms l))
    (haxioms : AxiomsProperty T S axioms ℕ Q)
    (hgeneralization : ∀ z : Obj T ℕ (V ⨯ S.algebra.a.2),
      Q ((F T ℕ).map (prod.snd : V ⨯ S.algebra.a.2 ⟶ S.algebra.a.2) z) →
        Q ((F T ℕ).map S.quantifierConstructor z))
    (hmodusPonens : ∀ z : Obj T ℕ (S.algebra.a.2 ⨯ S.algebra.a.2),
      Q ((F T ℕ).map (prod.fst : S.algebra.a.2 ⨯ S.algebra.a.2 ⟶ S.algebra.a.2) z) →
      Q ((F T ℕ).map S.implicationConstructor z) →
      Q ((F T ℕ).map (prod.snd : S.algebra.a.2 ⨯ S.algebra.a.2 ⟶ S.algebra.a.2) z)) :
    ConclusionProperty T S axioms ℕ Q :=
  firstOrder_conclusion_induction T S.logicalAxioms S.sentenceInclusion axioms
    S.quantifierConstructor S.implicationConstructor Q hlogical haxioms
    hgeneralization hmodusPonens

Lemma 7.7 lem:ultrapower-regular
The ultrapower functor U : Set ⟶ Set preserves finite limits, finite coproducts and regular images.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.Ultrapower.functor Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46Ultrapower.lean:40
def functor : Type u ⥤ Type u where

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.Ultrapower.regular Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46Ultrapower.lean:301
theorem regular : PreservesFiniteLimits (functor U) ∧
    PreservesFiniteCoproducts (functor U) ∧
    ∀ (X Y : Type u) (f : X ⟶ Y),
      ∃ e : (functor U).obj (image f) ≅ image ((functor U).map f),
        e.hom ≫ image.ι ((functor U).map f) = (functor U).map (image.ι f) :=
  ⟨inferInstance, inferInstance, fun _ _ f => ⟨regularImageIso U f, regularImageIso_hom_ι U f⟩⟩


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.Ultrapower.subtypeEquiv Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46Ultrapower.lean:104
def subtypeEquiv {X : Type u} (p : X → Prop) :
    Obj U {x // p x} ≃ {x : Obj U X // Germ.LiftPred p x} :=
  Equiv.ofBijective
    (fun x => ⟨Germ.map Subtype.val x, (subtype_range U p _).mp ⟨x, rfl⟩⟩)
    ⟨fun x y h => map_injective U Subtype.val Subtype.val_injective (congrArg Subtype.val h),
      fun x => by
        obtain ⟨y, hy⟩ := (subtype_range U p x.val).mpr x.property
        exact ⟨y, Subtype.ext hy⟩⟩

@[simp] theorem subtypeEquiv_val {X : Type u} (p : X → Prop) (x : Obj U {x // p x}) :
    (subtypeEquiv U p x).val = Germ.map Subtype.val x := rfl


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.Ultrapower.subtypeEquiv_naturality Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46Ultrapower.lean:118
theorem subtypeEquiv_naturality {X Y : Type u} (p : X → Prop) (q : Y → Prop)
    (f : X → Y) (hf : ∀ x, p x → q (f x)) (a : Obj U {x // p x}) :
    (subtypeEquiv U q (Germ.map (fun x : {x // p x} => ⟨f x.val, hf x.val x.property⟩) a)).val =
      Germ.map f (subtypeEquiv U p a).val := by

      (functor U).map (image.ι f) :=

theorem eval_representative {n : ℕ} (φ : ArithmeticSemisentence n)
    (b : Fin n → I → ℕ) :
    φ.Evalb (M := Model U) (fun j => Germ.ofFun (b j)) ↔
      ∀ᶠ i in (U : Filter I), φ.Evalb (M := ℕ) (fun j => b j i) := by

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ArithmeticUltrapower.predicateEquiv Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46UltrapowerComparison.lean:38
def predicateEquiv (C : Carrier) :
    Ultrapower.Obj U (PredicateInterpretation.Obj ℕ C) ≃
      PredicateInterpretation.Obj (Model U) C :=
  (Ultrapower.subtypeEquiv U (fun n : ℕ => C.val.Evalb ![n])).trans
    { toFun := fun a => ⟨a.val, (predicate_eval U C a.val).mpr a.property⟩

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ArithmeticUltrapower.predicateComparison Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46UltrapowerComparison.lean:53
def predicateComparison :
    PredicateInterpretation.functor 𝗣𝗔 ℕ ⋙ Ultrapower.functor U ≅
      PredicateInterpretation.functor 𝗣𝗔 (Model U) :=
  NatIso.ofComponents (fun C => (predicateEquiv U C).toIso) (by
    intro C D f
    induction f using Quotient.inductionOn with | _ f =>
      ext a
      induction a using Germ.inductionOn with | _ a =>
        symm
        apply (PredicateInterpretation.value_eq_iff 𝗣𝗔 (Model U) f _ _).mpr
        change f.graph.val.Evalb
          ![Germ.ofFun (fun i => (a i).val),
            Germ.ofFun (fun i => (PredicateInterpretation.value 𝗣𝗔 ℕ f (a i)).val)]
        have h : ∀ᶠ i in (U : Filter I), f.graph.val.Evalb
            ![(a i).val, (PredicateInterpretation.value 𝗣𝗔 ℕ f (a i)).val] :=
          Filter.Eventually.of_forall (fun i => PredicateInterpretation.value_graph 𝗣𝗔 ℕ f (a i))
        have hl := eval_representative U f.graph.val
          ![fun i => (a i).val,
            fun i => (PredicateInterpretation.value 𝗣𝗔 ℕ f (a i)).val]
        simp only [germ_cons, coordinate_cons, Matrix.empty_eq] at hl
        exact hl.mpr h)


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ArithmeticUltrapower.comparison Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46UltrapowerComparison.lean:77
def comparison :
    PredicateInterpretation.totalFunctor 𝗣𝗔 ℕ ⋙ Ultrapower.functor U ≅
      PredicateInterpretation.totalFunctor 𝗣𝗔 (Model U) :=
  Functor.associator (SyntacticPredicates.predicateEquivalence 𝗣𝗔).inverse
      (PredicateInterpretation.functor 𝗣𝗔 ℕ) (Ultrapower.functor U) ≪≫
    Functor.isoWhiskerLeft (SyntacticPredicates.predicateEquivalence 𝗣𝗔).inverse
      (predicateComparison U)


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ArithmeticUltrapower.canonicalComparison Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46UltrapowerComparison.lean:94
def canonicalComparison :
    PredicateInterpretation.canonicalDomainFunctor 𝗣𝗔 ℕ ⋙ Ultrapower.functor U ≅
      PredicateInterpretation.canonicalDomainFunctor 𝗣𝗔 (Model U) :=
  (Functor.isoWhiskerRight (PredicateInterpretation.totalCanonicalComparison 𝗣𝗔 ℕ)
    (Ultrapower.functor U)).symm ≪≫ comparison U ≪≫
      PredicateInterpretation.totalCanonicalComparison 𝗣𝗔 (Model U)

        ((PredicateInterpretation.canonicalDomainFunctor 𝗣𝗔 ℕ).map f) ≫
        (canonicalComparison U).hom.app Y =
      (canonicalComparison U).hom.app X ≫
        (PredicateInterpretation.canonicalDomainFunctor 𝗣𝗔 (Model U)).map f :=
  (canonicalComparison U).hom.naturality f

Lemma 8.2 lem:arithmetic-proof-search

theorem printed_diagonal_partial_proof_search (Γ : Carrier) :
    ∃ l : omegaObject 𝗣𝗔 ⟶ coprod (omegaObject 𝗣𝗔) (omegaObject 𝗣𝗔),
      ∀ (M : Type) [ORingStructure M] [M↓[ℒₒᵣ] ⊧* 𝗣𝗔],
        (∀ n : M, ¬(
          (SyntacticGeneratedAxioms.literalTheoremCarrier 𝗣𝗔
            variableSymbols functionSymbols relationSymbols bound Γ).val.Evalb
              ![NamedArithmeticUnarySubstitution.value ArithmeticDiagonalCodes.alpha.val n] ∧
          (SyntacticGeneratedAxioms.literalTheoremCarrier 𝗣𝗔
            variableSymbols functionSymbols relationSymbols bound Γ).val.Evalb
              ![negationWord M (NamedArithmeticUnarySubstitution.value ArithmeticDiagonalCodes.alpha.val n)])) →
        Idem.pull ((SyntacticModel.modelFunctor 𝗣𝗔 M).map l ≫
          ((SyntacticModel.modelRangeFunctor 𝗣𝗔 M).comparison
            (omegaObject 𝗣𝗔) (omegaObject 𝗣𝗔)).inv)
          (summand0 (PartialFun.of M) (PartialFun.of M)) =
            setIdem {n : M | (SyntacticGeneratedAxioms.literalTheoremCarrier 𝗣𝗔
              variableSymbols functionSymbols relationSymbols bound Γ).val.Evalb
                ![negationWord M (NamedArithmeticUnarySubstitution.value ArithmeticDiagonalCodes.alpha.val n)]} ∧
        Idem.pull ((SyntacticModel.modelFunctor 𝗣𝗔 M).map l ≫
          ((SyntacticModel.modelRangeFunctor 𝗣𝗔 M).comparison
            (omegaObject 𝗣𝗔) (omegaObject 𝗣𝗔)).inv)
          (summand1 (PartialFun.of M) (PartialFun.of M)) =
            setIdem {n : M | (SyntacticGeneratedAxioms.literalTheoremCarrier 𝗣𝗔
              variableSymbols functionSymbols relationSymbols bound Γ).val.Evalb
                ![NamedArithmeticUnarySubstitution.value ArithmeticDiagonalCodes.alpha.val n]} :=
  printed_partial_proof_search Γ
    (NamedArithmeticUnarySubstitution.namedFormula ArithmeticDiagonalCodes.alpha.val)
    (NamedArithmeticUnarySubstitution.namedFormula_freeVariables ArithmeticDiagonalCodes.alpha.val)

theorem partial_lift (C D : Carrier) :
    ∃ ell : omegaObject 𝗣𝗔 ⟶ coprod (omegaObject 𝗣𝗔) (omegaObject 𝗣𝗔),
      ∀ (M : Type) [ORingStructure M] [M↓[ℒₒᵣ] ⊧* 𝗣𝗔],
        (∀ x : M, ¬(C.val.Evalb ![x] ∧ D.val.Evalb ![x])) →
        Idem.pull ((SyntacticModel.modelFunctor 𝗣𝗔 M).map ell)
          (summand0 (PartialFun.of M) (PartialFun.of M)) =
            setIdem {x : M | D.val.Evalb ![x]} ∧
        Idem.pull ((SyntacticModel.modelFunctor 𝗣𝗔 M).map ell)

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ArithmeticComparisonTarget.comparison_of_evidence_bounds Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46ArithmeticComparisonTarget.lean:44
theorem comparison_of_evidence_bounds
    (hΓ : ∀ s : Obj 𝗣𝗔 M sentencePredicate,
      ¬ ((theoremPredicate Γ).val.Evalb ![s.val] ∧
        (theoremPredicate Γ).val.Evalb ![NamedArithmeticProofSearch.negationWord M s.val]))
    (hzero : ∀ n : M, ArithmeticDiagonalCodes.alpha.val.Evalb ![n] →
      (NamedArithmeticProofSearch.positive Γ diagonalFormula).val.Evalb ![n])
    (hone : ∀ n : M, ArithmeticDiagonalCodes.beta.val.Evalb ![n] →
      (NamedArithmeticProofSearch.negative Γ diagonalFormula).val.Evalb ![n]) :
    ∃ g : omegaObject 𝗣𝗔 ⟶ omegaObject 𝗣𝗔, isTotal g ∧
      Idem.pull ((SyntacticModel.modelFunctor 𝗣𝗔 M).map g ≫
        ArithmeticLiteralCoordinates.thetaMap M diagonalFormula diagonalFormula_freeVariables) (ArithmeticLiteralCoordinates.theory M Γ) = ⊥ ∧
      Idem.pull ((SyntacticModel.modelFunctor 𝗣𝗔 M).map g ≫
        ArithmeticLiteralCoordinates.thetaMap M diagonalFormula diagonalFormula_freeVariables)
        (Idem.pull (ArithmeticLiteralCoordinates.negationMap M) (ArithmeticLiteralCoordinates.theory M Γ)) = ⊥ ∧
      ArithmeticLiteralCoordinates.theory M Γ ⊔ Idem.pull (ArithmeticLiteralCoordinates.negationMap M) (ArithmeticLiteralCoordinates.theory M Γ) ≠ ⊤ := by

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ArithmeticInternalComparison.internal_comparison Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46ArithmeticInternalComparison.lean:249
theorem internal_comparison :
    (ArithmeticDomainExtension.sentenceTheory A ⊔
      Idem.pull ArithmeticDomainExtension.sentenceNegation
        (ArithmeticDomainExtension.sentenceTheory A) ≠ ⊤) ∧
    ((ArithmeticDomainExtension.canonicalExtension ℕ).mapIdem
        (ArithmeticDomainExtension.sentenceTheory A) ⊔
      Idem.pull ((ArithmeticDomainExtension.canonicalExtension ℕ).toFunctor.map
        ArithmeticDomainExtension.sentenceNegation)
        ((ArithmeticDomainExtension.canonicalExtension ℕ).mapIdem
          (ArithmeticDomainExtension.sentenceTheory A)) ≠ ⊤) ∧
    (∀ (I : Type) (U : Ultrafilter I),
      (ArithmeticDomainExtension.canonicalExtension (ArithmeticUltrapower.Model U)).mapIdem
          (ArithmeticDomainExtension.sentenceTheory A) ⊔
        Idem.pull ((ArithmeticDomainExtension.canonicalExtension (ArithmeticUltrapower.Model U)).toFunctor.map
          ArithmeticDomainExtension.sentenceNegation)
          ((ArithmeticDomainExtension.canonicalExtension (ArithmeticUltrapower.Model U)).mapIdem
            (ArithmeticDomainExtension.sentenceTheory A)) ≠ ⊤) ∧
    (∃ g : omegaObject 𝗣𝗔 ⟶ omegaObject 𝗣𝗔, isTotal g ∧
      ∃ gamma : ℕ → ℕ,
        (∀ n : ℕ, gamma n ∈ (SyntacticModel.modelFunctor 𝗣𝗔 ℕ).map g n) ∧
        ∀ n : ℕ,
          let φ := NamedArithmeticDiagonalBounds.instanceFormula ArithmeticDiagonalCodes.alpha.val (gamma n)
          ArithmeticComparisonTarget.diagonalFormula.substitute 0 (numeral (gamma n)) = some φ ∧
          φ.freeVariables = [] ∧
          ¬ SyntacticNamedHilbert.Derives functionSymbols relationSymbols bound A φ ∧
          ¬ SyntacticNamedHilbert.Derives functionSymbols relationSymbols bound A (.neg φ)) :=
  ⟨source_incomplete A hPA hc,interpreted_standard_incomplete A hPA hc,
    (fun _ U => interpreted_ultrapower_incomplete A hPA hc U),total_undecidable_family A hPA hc⟩

theorem total_undecidable_family :
    ∃ g : omegaObject 𝗣𝗔 ⟶ omegaObject 𝗣𝗔, isTotal g ∧
      ∃ gamma : ℕ → ℕ,
        (∀ n : ℕ, gamma n ∈ (SyntacticModel.modelFunctor 𝗣𝗔 ℕ).map g n) ∧
        ∀ n : ℕ,
          let φ := NamedArithmeticDiagonalBounds.instanceFormula ArithmeticDiagonalCodes.alpha.val (gamma n)
          ArithmeticComparisonTarget.diagonalFormula.substitute 0 (numeral (gamma n)) = some φ ∧
          φ.freeVariables = [] ∧
          ¬ SyntacticNamedHilbert.Derives functionSymbols relationSymbols bound A φ ∧
          ¬ SyntacticNamedHilbert.Derives functionSymbols relationSymbols bound A (.neg φ) := by

Corollary 8.4 cor:arithmetic
Theorem/refutation sets of every consistent c.e. PA extension are recursively inseparable; all consistent c.e. extensions are incomplete.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ArithmeticSentenceInseparability.no_recursive_separator Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46ArithmeticSentenceInseparabilityCore.lean:124
theorem no_recursive_separator (A : SyntacticPredicates.Carrier)
    (hPA : ArithmeticNamedAxiomComparison.ExtendsPA A) :
    ¬ ∃ R : Set ℕ, ComputablePred (· ∈ R) ∧ theoremSet A ⊆ R ∧
      Disjoint R (refutationSet A) :=
  no_recursive_separator_of_bounds A NamedArithmeticDiagonalBounds.namedAlpha
    NamedArithmeticDiagonalBounds.namedAlpha_freeVariables
    (NamedArithmeticDiagonalBounds.standard_positive_of_axioms_admissible A
      (ArithmeticNamedAxiomComparison.axiomsAdmissible_PA A hPA))
    (NamedArithmeticDiagonalBounds.standard_negative_of_axioms_admissible A
      (ArithmeticNamedAxiomComparison.axiomsAdmissible_PA A hPA))


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ArithmeticSentenceInseparability.recursively_inseparable Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46ArithmeticSentenceInseparability.lean:84
theorem recursively_inseparable (A : SyntacticPredicates.Carrier)
    (hPA : ArithmeticNamedAxiomComparison.ExtendsPA A)
    (hc : Entailment.Consistent (ArithmeticNamedProofConnectives.system A)) :
    Numerical.RecursivelyInseparable (theoremSet A) (refutationSet A) :=
  ⟨Set.disjoint_left.mpr (fun word hp hn =>
      NamedGammaEvidenceSoundness.consistent_standard_word_disjoint A hPA hc word
        ⟨carrier_eval_transport (theoremCarrier_variables_eq A) word
            (eval_literal_set _ word hp),
          carrier_eval_transport (refutationCarrier_variables_eq A) word
            (eval_literal_set _ word hn)⟩),
    no_recursive_separator A hPA⟩


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ArithmeticSentenceInseparability.sentence_incomplete Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46ArithmeticSentenceInseparability.lean:180
theorem sentence_incomplete (A : SyntacticPredicates.Carrier)
    (hPA : ArithmeticNamedAxiomComparison.ExtendsPA A)
    (hc : Entailment.Consistent (ArithmeticNamedProofConnectives.system A)) :
    ∃ φ : NamedArithmeticSignature.Formula, φ.freeVariables = [] ∧
      ¬ D A φ ∧ ¬ D A (.neg φ) :=
  sentence_incomplete_of_bounds A NamedArithmeticDiagonalBounds.namedAlpha
    NamedArithmeticDiagonalBounds.namedAlpha_freeVariables
    (recursively_inseparable A hPA hc).1
    (NamedArithmeticDiagonalBounds.standard_positive_of_axioms_admissible A
      (ArithmeticNamedAxiomComparison.axiomsAdmissible_PA A hPA))
    (NamedArithmeticDiagonalBounds.standard_negative_of_axioms_admissible A
      (ArithmeticNamedAxiomComparison.axiomsAdmissible_PA A hPA))

theorem extension_sentence_incomplete (A B : SyntacticPredicates.Carrier)
    (hPA : ArithmeticNamedAxiomComparison.ExtendsPA A)

      A.val.Evalb (M := ℕ) ![formulaCode φ] → φ.freeVariables = [] → D B φ)

      ¬ D B φ ∧ ¬ D B (.neg φ) :=

Proposition 8.5 prop:arithmetic-theory-fibers
Every numeral pullback of the actual family conclusion is an initial inference algebra; the sentence square is cartesian and its image is the Γ_e sentence theory.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ArithmeticIndexedFibers.fiberInitial Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46ArithmeticIndexedFibers.lean:81
def fiberInitial : IsInitial (fiberAlgebra AxPA e) :=
  ArithmeticIndexedAlgebraSplit.productInitialFirst _
    (IsInitial.isInitialObj (algebraEquivalence AxPA e).functor
      (ArithmeticIndexedFamily.algebra AxPA) (ArithmeticIndexedFamily.initial AxPA))

    ((formulaOccurrenceComparison_iff (E) V σ π d (S₀) (S₁)
      (literalListsEquality V σ π d) (literalAlgebraIso V σ π d)).mp
        (literal_formulaOccurrences_comparison V σ π d))
    (syntaxList_nil_comparison (E) V σ π d (S₀) (S₁) (literalListsEquality V σ π d))

Lemma 8.9 lem:arithmetic-signed-theory
The free first-order theory generated by the arithmetic diagonal pair is internally consistent and essentially incomplete.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ArithmeticComplementaryInclusions.conditional Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46ArithmeticComplementaryInclusions.lean:46
theorem conditional (A B : Subobject (identityOmega 𝗣𝗔)) (h : IsCompl A B) :
    ∃ (c : (A : TotalSplit 𝗣𝗔) ⨿ (B : TotalSplit 𝗣𝗔) ≅ identityOmega 𝗣𝗔)
      (ℓ : identityOmega 𝗣𝗔 ⟶ numberSum),
      c.hom = coprod.desc A.arrow B.arrow ∧
      ℓ = c.inv ≫ coprod.desc (A.arrow ≫ rightInjection) (B.arrow ≫ leftInjection) ∧
      A.arrow ≫ ℓ = A.arrow ≫ rightInjection ∧ B.arrow ≫ ℓ = B.arrow ≫ leftInjection ∧
      isTotal ℓ.hom.hom := by

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ArithmeticSignedTheory.printed_statement Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46ArithmeticSignedTheory.lean:32
theorem printed_statement : Entailment.Consistent 𝗣𝗔 →
    Inseparable (SplitObject.includedIdem ArithmeticDiagonalCodes.a0)
      (SplitObject.includedIdem ArithmeticDiagonalCodes.a1) ∧
    ((ArithmeticSignedTotal.theory presentation).val ⊓
      (Subobject.pullback (ArithmeticSignedTotal.negation presentation)).obj
        (ArithmeticSignedTotal.theory presentation).val = ⊥) ∧
    ∀ U : FirstOrderReflection.Theory (ArithmeticSignedTotal.proofs presentation),
      (ArithmeticSignedTotal.theory presentation).val ≤ U.val →
      U.val ⊓ (Subobject.pullback (ArithmeticSignedTotal.negation presentation)).obj U.val = ⊥ →
      U.val ⊔ (Subobject.pullback (ArithmeticSignedTotal.negation presentation)).obj U.val ≠ ⊤ :=
  fun _ => ⟨ArithmeticDiagonalSplitting.source_pair_inseparable,
    ArithmeticSignedTotal.essentially_incomplete presentation⟩

    (a : A ⟶ (realization 𝗣𝗔).obj (presentation.syntaxData N).sentenceObject) :
    ((FirstOrderReflection.presentationGeneration (ArithmeticSignedTotal.proofs presentation)
      (ArithmeticSignedTotal.initial presentation)).obj (Over.mk a)).val =

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.Miller.Uniform.J Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46MillerCategoryRelative.lean:189
def J (z : Point) (p : Bits) (hp : p ∈ representations z) :
    RangeFunctor (PointObject z) (RelativeObject p) :=
  restrictFamilyRangeFunctor (P := Family.ofPoint z) (Q := Family.singleton p)
    (fun q hq => by
      have hqp : q=p := Set.mem_singleton_iff.mp hq
      subst q
      exact hp)

instance (z : Point) (p : Bits) (hp : p ∈ representations z) : (J z p hp).toFunctor.Faithful :=
  inferInstanceAs ((restrictFamily (P := Family.ofPoint z) (Q := Family.singleton p)
    (fun q hq => by
      have hqp : q=p := Set.mem_singleton_iff.mp hq
      subst q
      exact hp)).Faithful)

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.Miller.Cut.uniform_cut_reduction Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46MillerCutReduction.lean:77
theorem uniform_cut_reduction :
    ∃ h : ℕ → ℕ,Computable h ∧ ∀ a : Point,IsMiller a →
      h ⁻¹' CutDigits.lowerCut a = {e | ∃ m,m ∈ Unanimity.tau a e e ∧ m%2=0} ∧
      h ⁻¹' CutDigits.upperCut a = {e | ∃ m,m ∈ Unanimity.tau a e e ∧ m%2=1} :=
  ⟨reduction,reduction_computable,fun a ha =>
    ⟨Set.ext (reduction_lower a ha),Set.ext (reduction_upper a ha)⟩⟩

theorem printed_cut_reduction (a : Point) (ha : IsMiller a) :
    ∃ h : ℕ → ℕ,Computable h ∧
      h ⁻¹' CutDigits.lowerCut a = {e | ∃ m,m ∈ Unanimity.tau a e e ∧ m%2=0} ∧
      h ⁻¹' CutDigits.upperCut a = {e | ∃ m,m ∈ Unanimity.tau a e e ∧ m%2=1} :=
  ⟨reduction,reduction_computable,Set.ext (reduction_lower a ha),Set.ext (reduction_upper a ha)⟩


Corollary 9.4 cor:miller-cuts-inseparable

def lowerIdem (z : Point) : Idem
    (Uniform.N (Uniform.Family.ofPoint z)) :=
  Uniform.setIdem (Uniform.N (Uniform.Family.ofPoint z)) (lowerCut z) (lowerCut_enumerable z)


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.Miller.CutDigits.upperIdem Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46MillerCutDigitsStrict.lean:178
def upperIdem (z : Point) : Idem
    (Uniform.N (Uniform.Family.ofPoint z)) :=
  Uniform.setIdem (Uniform.N (Uniform.Family.ofPoint z)) (upperCut z) (upperCut_enumerable z)

  Uniform.support_setIdem _ _ _

@[simp] theorem upperIdem_support (z : Point) : Uniform.support (upperIdem z) = upperCut z :=
  Uniform.support_setIdem _ _ _


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.Miller.CutDigits.fixed_representation_separator Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46MillerCutDigits.lean:147
theorem fixed_representation_separator (z : Point) (p : Bits) (hp : p ∈ representations z) :
    lowerCut z ⊆ separator p ∧ Disjoint (separator p) (upperCut z) ∧
      Nat.RecursiveIn (OracleComputation.oracleSet (OracleRelative.oracleSupport p))
        (fun n => Part.some (decision p n)) :=
  ⟨(separates_point z p hp).1,(separates_point z p hp).2,decision_recursiveIn p⟩


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.Miller.CutInseparability.source_diagonal_inseparable Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46MillerCutInseparability.lean:164
theorem source_diagonal_inseparable (z : Point) :
    Inseparable (diagonal0 z) (diagonal1 z) :=
  turing_pair_inseparable (Uniform.tau z) (Uniform.tau_isTuring z)


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.Miller.Cut.printed_cut_inseparability Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46MillerCutApplications.lean:43
theorem printed_cut_inseparability (a : Point) (ha : IsMiller a) :
    Inseparable (CutDigits.lowerIdem a) (CutDigits.upperIdem a) :=
  CutInseparability.cuts_inseparable_of_recursive_reduction a reduction reduction_computable
    (reduction_support0 a ha) (reduction_support1 a ha)


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.Miller.Cut.printed_cut_contrast Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46MillerCutApplications.lean:59
theorem printed_cut_contrast (a : Point) (ha : IsMiller a) :
    Inseparable (CutDigits.lowerIdem a) (CutDigits.upperIdem a) ∧
      ∀ p ∈ representations a,
        CutDigits.lowerCut a ⊆ CutDigits.separator p ∧
          Disjoint (CutDigits.separator p) (CutDigits.upperCut a) ∧
          Nat.RecursiveIn (OracleComputation.oracleSet (OracleRelative.oracleSupport p))
            (fun n => Part.some (CutDigits.decision p n)) :=
  ⟨printed_cut_inseparability a ha,fixed_representation_separator a⟩


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.Miller.Cut.mapped_tau_notTuring Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46MillerCutApplications.lean:71
theorem mapped_tau_notTuring (a : Point) (ha : IsMiller a)

    ¬RecursionCategory.IsTuringMorphism ((Uniform.J a p hp).toFunctor.map (Uniform.tau a)) :=
  CutInseparability.mapped_tau_notTuring_of_recursive_reduction a reduction reduction_computable
    (reduction_support0 a ha) (reduction_support1 a ha) p hp

    ∃ r : (S).sentenceObject.obj ⟶ (image (sentenceConclusion z V σ π d axioms)).obj,

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.Miller.Derivation.Parameterized.fold_underlying_unique Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46MillerSliceDerivations.lean:60
theorem fold_underlying_unique (B : Endofunctor.Algebra R.inference)
    (f : (algebra z R).a.left ⟶ B.a.left)
    (hf : f ≫ B.a.hom = (algebra z R).a.hom)
    (hc : R.inference.map (Over.homMk f hf) ≫ B.str =
      (algebra z R).str ≫ Over.homMk f hf) : f = (fold z R B).f.left :=
  congrArg (fun h : algebra z R ⟶ B => h.f.left)
    ((initial z R).hom_ext (⟨Over.homMk f hf, hc⟩ : algebra z R ⟶ B) (fold z R B))

Theorem 9.7 thm:miller-incompleteness
In the ordered-field language with constants cₙ, T_a is the deductive closure of the real closed ordered field axioms and the strict rational bounds on cₙ. Every consistent extension in that language whose axioms are semidecided by one program under every binary name of a has inseparable theorem and refutation domains and is incomplete.

          M.relations=Semantics.relations (K := K) := by

theorem finite_proof_transport_iff_theoremIdem_le
    (m : A ⟶ (S₂).sentenceObject) [Mono m] :
    (∀ φ : OrderedFieldSyntax.Formula,
      RealClosedBounds.Proof (RealClosedAxioms.theoryAxioms a) φ →
        RealClosedBounds.Proof (recoveredCodes a m) φ) ↔
      CanonicalIncompleteness.theoremIdem a (RealClosedAxioms.theoryAxioms a)
        (RealClosedAxioms.theoryAxioms_enumerable a) ≤ theoremIdem a m :=

    (extends_iff_theoremIdem_le a m)

theorem printed_categorical_incompleteness (m : A ⟶ (S₂).sentenceObject) [Mono m]
    (ha : IsMiller a)

    (hcons : theoremIdem a m⊓Idem.pull (S₂).sentenceNegation.hom (theoremIdem a m)=⊥) :

theorem printed_essential_incompleteness_of_consistent
    (m : A ⟶ (S₂).sentenceObject) [Mono m]
    (ha : IsMiller a) (hext : RealClosedBounds.Extends a (recoveredCodes a m))
    (hcons : TheoryDomainApplication.Consistent (recoveredCodes a m)) :
    Inseparable (theoremIdem a m) (refutationIdem a m) ∧
      theoremIdem a m⊔refutationIdem a m≠⊤ :=
  printed_essential_incompleteness a m ha hext ((consistent_iff_disjoint a m).mp hcons)

theorem printed_native_incomplete_of_consistent
    (m : A ⟶ (S₂).sentenceObject) [Mono m]
    (ha : IsMiller a) (hext : RealClosedBounds.Extends a (recoveredCodes a m))
    (hcons : TheoryDomainApplication.Consistent (recoveredCodes a m)) :
    ∃ φ : OrderedFieldSyntax.Formula,φ.freeVariables=[] ∧
      ¬RealClosedBounds.Proof (recoveredCodes a m) φ ∧
      ¬RealClosedBounds.Proof (recoveredCodes a m) (.neg φ) :=
  printed_native_incomplete a m ha hext ((consistent_iff_disjoint a m).mp hcons)


Corollary 9.8 cor:miller-associated-categories
Every consistent internal extension of the expanded Miller field theory satisfies the associated-category theorem and has the no-total-extension witness.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.Miller.AssociatedData.point_factors Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46MillerAssociatedData.lean:100
theorem point_factors (U : Theory a) :
    ∃ p : pointDomain a ⟶ (U.val : Total.CanonicalTotal a), p ≫ U.val.arrow = theoremPoint a :=
  NullaryLogicalPoint.factors (syntaxData a).logicalAxioms (syntaxData a).sentenceInclusion
    (syntaxData a).quantifierConstructor (syntaxData a).implicationConstructor (proofs a)
    (theoremPoint a) (logicalPoint a) (logicalPoint_diagram a) U

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.Miller.AssociatedIncompleteness.essentially_incomplete Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46MillerAssociatedIncompleteness.lean:76
theorem essentially_incomplete (ha : IsMiller a) (hExt : base a ≤ U.val)
    (hc : U.val ⊓ (refutation a U) = ⊥) :
    ((constantTheory a U).val ⊓ (Subobject.pullback
      ((constantFamilyFunctor (U.val : Total.CanonicalTotal a)).map
        (syntaxData a).sentenceNegation)).obj (constantTheory a U).val = ⊥) ∧
    ∀ W : FirstOrderReflection.Theory (sliceProofs a (U.val : Total.CanonicalTotal a)),
      (constantTheory a U).val ≤ W.val →
      W.val ⊓ (Subobject.pullback ((constantFamilyFunctor (U.val : Total.CanonicalTotal a)).map
        (syntaxData a).sentenceNegation)).obj W.val = ⊥ →
      W.val ⊔ (Subobject.pullback ((constantFamilyFunctor (U.val : Total.CanonicalTotal a)).map
        (syntaxData a).sentenceNegation)).obj W.val ≠ ⊤ :=
  ⟨SliceSubobjectPredicates.constant_consistency (U.val : Total.CanonicalTotal a)
    (syntaxData a).sentenceNegation U.val hc,
    every_consistent_extension_incomplete a U ha hExt⟩

theorem actual_essentially_incomplete (ha : IsMiller a) (hExt : base a ≤ U.val)
    (hc : U.val ⊓ (refutation a U) = ⊥) :
    (actualTheoryDomain a U ⊓ (Subobject.pullback (actualNegation a U)).obj
      (actualTheoryDomain a U) = ⊥) ∧
    ∀ W : Subobject ((PartialMaps.totalEquivalence
        (D := Over (U.val : Total.CanonicalTotal a))).functor.obj
          (constantFamily (U.val : Total.CanonicalTotal a) (syntaxData a).sentenceObject)),
      actualTheoryDomain a U ≤ W →
      W ⊓ (Subobject.pullback (actualNegation a U)).obj W = ⊥ →
      W ⊔ (Subobject.pullback (actualNegation a U)).obj W ≠ ⊤ := by

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ForkInference.inferenceIso Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46ForkDerivationInference.lean:211
def inferenceIso : (rules axioms implication).inference ≅ printedH axioms implication :=
  NatIso.ofComponents (inputIso axioms implication) (by
    intro Q Q' h
    apply Over.OverMorphism.ext
    change Limits.Sigma.map ((rules axioms implication).ruleMap h) ≫
        toPrintedLeft axioms implication Q' =
      toPrintedLeft axioms implication Q ≫ coprod.map (𝟙 Ax) (mpMap implication h)
    apply Sigma.hom_ext
    intro i
    fin_cases i
    · erw [Sigma.ι_map_assoc,toPrintedLeft_ι_zero,toPrintedLeft_ι_zero_assoc,
        coprod.inl_map,Category.id_comp,RulePresentation.ruleMap_fst_assoc]
      rfl
    · exact (unaryInputInitial axioms implication Q).hom_ext _ _
    · erw [Sigma.ι_map_assoc,toPrintedLeft_ι_two,toPrintedLeft_ι_two_assoc,coprod.inr_map]
      rfl)

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ForkDerivationImages.least_theorem_image Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46ForkDerivationImages.lean:101
theorem least_theorem_image (a b : Idem V) :
    ClosedUnder (ForkDerivation.rules a b).inference (image.ι (conclusion a b)) ∧
      ∀ {U : E} (m : U ⟶ S) [Mono m],
        ClosedUnder (ForkDerivation.rules a b).inference m →
          ∃ w : image (conclusion a b) ⟶ U,
            w ≫ m = image.ι (conclusion a b) :=
  (ForkDerivation.rules a b).least_theorem_subobject
    (ForkDerivation.inferenceAlgebra a b) (ForkDerivation.inferenceInitial a b)

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ForkDerivationImages.proof_presentation Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46ForkDerivationImages.lean:208
theorem proof_presentation (a b : Idem V) (hd : a ⊓ b = ⊥) :
    IsLeast {P : Subobject S |
      ClosedUnder (ForkDerivation.rules a b).inference P.arrow}
      (theoremSubobject a b) ∧
    IsLeast {e | ForkSyntax.IsTheory a b e} (theoremRange a b) ∧
      a ≤ Idem.pull ForkSyntax.theta (theoremRange a b) ∧
      b ≤ Idem.pull ForkSyntax.theta (Idem.pull ForkSyntax.nu (theoremRange a b)) ∧
      theoremRange a b ⊓ Idem.pull ForkSyntax.nu (theoremRange a b) = ⊥ := by

    n ∈ support (rightIdem (t0 a b)) ↔
      Formula.decode n ∈ Theorems (support (rightIdem a)) (support (rightIdem b)) :=
  conclusion_range (support (rightIdem a)) (support (rightIdem b)) _ _ n


Theorem 10.5 thm:cosheaf-observation
Consistent extension families give internally inseparable theorem/refutation idempotents, recursively inseparable N² observations, covariant extension functor, and EACH endpoint theory incomplete.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.LocalConnectedness.ForkSyntax.cosheaf_observation Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/ForkObservationFunctor.lean:131
theorem cosheaf_observation (i : Bool) (t : Theory) :
    Inseparable t.val (Idem.pull nu t.val) ∧
      (REPred (· ∈ tuplePositiveSet i t.val) ∧ REPred (· ∈ tupleNegativeSet i t.val)) ∧
      (Disjoint (tuplePositiveSet i t.val) (tupleNegativeSet i t.val) ∧
        ¬ ∃ D : Set (ℕ × ℕ),ComputablePred (· ∈ D) ∧ tuplePositiveSet i t.val ⊆ D ∧
          Disjoint D (tupleNegativeSet i t.val)) ∧ t.val ⊔ Idem.pull nu t.val≠⊤ :=
  ⟨(coded_observation i t).1,tuple_observations_enumerable i t.val,
    tuple_observations_inseparable i t,(coded_observation i t).2.2⟩

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.LocalConnectedness.ForkSyntax.cosheaf_observation_v46 Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46Applications.lean:123
theorem cosheaf_observation_v46 (i : Bool) (t : Theory) :
    Inseparable t.val (Idem.pull nu t.val) ∧
      (REPred (· ∈ tuplePositiveSet i t.val) ∧
        REPred (· ∈ tupleNegativeSet i t.val)) ∧
      (Disjoint (tuplePositiveSet i t.val) (tupleNegativeSet i t.val) ∧
        ¬ ∃ D : Set (ℕ × ℕ), ComputablePred (· ∈ D) ∧
          tuplePositiveSet i t.val ⊆ D ∧ Disjoint D (tupleNegativeSet i t.val)) ∧
      ({x : ℕ × ℕ | Nat.pair x.1 x.2 ∈ support EffectiveFork.endpointPair0} ⊆
          tuplePositiveSet i t.val ∧
        {x : ℕ × ℕ | Nat.pair x.1 x.2 ∈ support EffectiveFork.endpointPair1} ⊆
          tupleNegativeSet i t.val) ∧
      endpointTheory i t.val ⊔
        Idem.pull (natArrow negCode negCode_computable) (endpointTheory i t.val) ≠ ⊤ :=
  ⟨(essential_incompleteness_v46 t.val t.property.1 t.property.2.1).1,
    tuple_observations_enumerable i t.val, tuple_observations_inseparable i t,
    tuple_observed_bounds i t.val t.property.1, endpoint_theory_incomplete i t⟩

theorem underlying_unique (N : C) {D E P Q : C} (b : P ⟶ Q) (h : D ⟶ E)
    (B : Endofunctor.Algebra (polynomial N E Q))
    (f g : (algebra N D P).a.left ⟶ B.a.left)
    (hfP : f ≫ B.a.hom = (algebra N D P).a.hom ≫ b)
    (hgP : g ≫ B.a.hom = (algebra N D P).a.hom ≫ b)
    (hf : (comparison N b h).app (algebra N D P).a ≫
      (polynomial N E Q).map (Over.homMk f hfP) ≫ B.str =
        (Over.map b).map (algebra N D P).str ≫ Over.homMk f hfP)
    (hg : (comparison N b h).app (algebra N D P).a ≫
      (polynomial N E Q).map (Over.homMk g hgP) ≫ B.str =
        (Over.map b).map (algebra N D P).str ≫ Over.homMk g hgP) : f = g :=
  congrArg Over.Hom.left (unique N b h B (Over.homMk f hfP) (Over.homMk g hgP) hf hg)

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.SlicePolynomialComparison.jointIso Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46SlicePolynomialComparison.lean:44
def jointIso (p q : SyntaxPolynomial I) (j : J) :
    SyntaxPolynomial.jointFunctor (p.mapCoefficients (globalCoefficients c P))
        (q.mapCoefficients (globalCoefficients c P)) ⋙
        (SliceEvaluation.componentFunctor P j).prod (SliceEvaluation.componentFunctor P j) ≅
      ((SliceEvaluation.componentFunctor P j).prod (SliceEvaluation.componentFunctor P j)) ⋙
        SyntaxPolynomial.jointFunctor (p.mapCoefficients (localCoefficients c P j))
          (q.mapCoefficients (localCoefficients c P j)) :=
  NatIso.ofComponents (fun X => Iso.prod (asIso (component c P p X j))
    (asIso (component c P q X j))) (by
      intro X Y m
      exact Prod.ext
        (LaxPolynomialComparison.naturality (SliceEvaluation.componentFunctor P j)
          (fun i => (SliceEvaluation.coefficientIso P (c i) j).hom) p m)
        (LaxPolynomialComparison.naturality (SliceEvaluation.componentFunctor P j)
          (fun i => (SliceEvaluation.coefficientIso P (c i) j).hom) q m))

omit [HasFiniteCoproducts C] in

theorem transition_square (p : SyntaxPolynomial I) (X : Over P × Over P)
    {j k : J} (f : j ⟶ k) :
    SliceEvaluation.transition P ((p.mapCoefficients (globalCoefficients c P)).eval.obj X) f ≫
        component c P p X k =
      (Over.map (P.map f)).map (component c P p X j) ≫ input c P p X f :=
  LaxPolynomialComparison.natural_square (SliceEvaluation.componentFunctor P j)
    (fun i => (SliceEvaluation.coefficientIso P (c i) j).hom)
    (SliceEvaluation.componentFunctor P k) (Over.map (P.map f))
    (fun i => (SliceEvaluation.coefficientIso P (c i) k).hom)
    (fun i => SliceEvaluation.coefficientTransition P (c i) f)
    (Over.postMap (X := P) ((evaluation J C).map f))

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.FiniteInferenceEvaluation.comparison Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46FiniteInferenceEvaluation.lean:68
def comparison : R.inference ⋙ Over.post K ≅ Over.post K ⋙ (mappedRules K R).inference :=
  NatIso.ofComponents (overIso K R) (by
    intro Q Q' f
    apply Over.OverMorphism.ext
    change K.map (Limits.Sigma.map (R.ruleMap f)) ≫ (sumIso K R Q').hom =
      (sumIso K R Q).hom ≫ Limits.Sigma.map ((mappedRules K R).ruleMap ((Over.post K).map f))
    apply mapSum_hom_ext K (R.ruleObject Q)
    intro i
    rw [← K.map_comp_assoc, Sigma.ι_map, K.map_comp_assoc, sumIso_iota_assoc,
      sumIso_iota, ← Category.assoc, branch_natural, Category.assoc]
    simp only [Sigma.ι_map])

      rst (decision U hd) = (domain MonicPartialSliceComparison.inverses (a ⊔ b)).hom ∧
      a.arrow.hom ≫ decision U hd =
        (Two.constant (a : PartialMaps.Tot (Over (U.val : Object))) true).hom ∧

        (Two.constant (b : PartialMaps.Tot (Over (U.val : Object))) false).hom ∧
      ¬ RestrictionRetraction.HasTotalExtension (decision U hd) := by

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.SyntaxPolynomial.jointFunctor Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46CategoricalSyntax.lean:87
def jointFunctor (p q : SyntaxPolynomial D) : D × D ⥤ D × D :=
  p.eval.prod' q.eval


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.SyntaxPolynomial.termExpression Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46CategoricalSyntax.lean:71
def termExpression (V : D) (σ : ℕ → D) (d : ℕ) : SyntaxPolynomial D :=
  .sum (.coefficient V) (constructors σ d)


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.SyntaxPolynomial.formulaExpression Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46CategoricalSyntax.lean:74
def formulaExpression (V : D) (π : ℕ → D) (d : ℕ) : SyntaxPolynomial D :=
  .sum (.sum (.sum (.sum (power .term 2) (constructors π d)) .formula)
    (power .formula 2)) (.product (.coefficient V) .formula)

@[simp] theorem eval_power_zero (p : SyntaxPolynomial D) (X : D × D) :
    (power p 0).eval.obj X = ⊤_ D := rfl

@[simp] theorem eval_power_one (p : SyntaxPolynomial D) (X : D × D) :
    (power p 1).eval.obj X = p.eval.obj X := rfl

@[simp] theorem eval_power_two (p : SyntaxPolynomial D) (X : D × D) :
    (power p 2).eval.obj X = (p.eval.obj X ⨯ p.eval.obj X) := rfl

      constantFamily S.substitutionParameters S.algebra.a.2) ⟶ S.substitutionAlgebra.a)

      ((SyntaxPolynomial.termExpression V σ d).withParameters S.substitutionParameters)

    f = S.substitutionFold :=

Theorem A.5 thm:least-theorem-subobject
The regular image of an initial inference-algebra conclusion is the least closed subobject.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.RulePresentation.least_theorem_subobject Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46Inference.lean:247
theorem least_theorem_subobject (A : Endofunctor.Algebra R.inference)
    (hA : IsInitial A) :
    ClosedUnder R.inference (image.ι A.a.hom) ∧
      ∀ {U : D} (m : U ⟶ F) [Mono m], ClosedUnder R.inference m →
        ∃ w : image A.a.hom ⟶ U, w ≫ m = image.ι A.a.hom :=
  least_closed_image R.inference A R.inference_preservesRegularEpis hA

Theorem A.6 thm:first-order-theory
Initial H_A gives least first-order generated theory; sentence image equals the pullback of its formula image.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.CategoricalSyntax.generated_firstOrder_theory Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46FirstOrderTheory.lean:28
theorem generated_firstOrder_theory {A : D} (axioms : A ⟶ S.sentenceObject)
    (P : Endofunctor.Algebra (S.logicalRules axioms).inference) (hP : IsInitial P) :
    ClosedUnder (S.logicalRules axioms).inference (image.ι P.a.hom) ∧
      (∀ {T : D} (m : T ⟶ S.algebra.a.2) [Mono m],
        ClosedUnder (S.logicalRules axioms).inference m →
          ∃ w : image P.a.hom ⟶ T, w ≫ m = image.ι P.a.hom) ∧
      (∃ a : S.logicalParameters ⨿ A ⟶ image P.a.hom,
        a ≫ image.ι P.a.hom = coprod.desc S.logicalAxioms
          (axioms ≫ S.sentenceInclusion)) ∧
      imageSubobject (pullback.snd P.a.hom S.sentenceInclusion) =
        (Subobject.pullback S.sentenceInclusion).obj (imageSubobject P.a.hom) := by

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.CategoricalSyntax.logicalAxioms Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46LogicalSchemes.lean:180
def logicalAxioms : S.logicalParameters ⟶ S.algebra.a.2 :=

    (coprod.desc (coprod.desc S.hilbertOne S.hilbertTwo) S.hilbertThree)
      S.hilbertInstantiation) S.hilbertDistribution) S.hilbertReflexivity) S.hilbertEquality

    S.quantifierConstructor S.implicationConstructor

theorem span_range {X Y : Object} (s : PartialSpans.Span X Y) :
    Ranges.range s.value = Ranges.range s.map.hom :=
  Ranges.precomp_split_epi (PartialSpans.inverse s.mono) s.mono.hom
    (PartialSpans.hom_inverse s.mono) s.map.hom

theorem range_product {W X Y Z : Object} (f : W ⟶ X) (g : Y ⟶ Z) :
    (Ranges.range (productMap f g)).hom=
      productMap (Ranges.range f).hom (Ranges.range g).hom :=
  Hom.ext (EnumerableCarriers.range_product f.center g.center)
    (EnumerableCarriers.range_product f.left g.left)
    (EnumerableCarriers.range_product f.right g.right)

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.V46.ForkTupleEvaluation.left_graph Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46ForkTupleTuringPoints.lean:97
theorem left_graph (k p l x a b : ℕ) :
    Graph (evaluatedTau .left) (pairPoint (point₂ k p) (point₂ l x)) (point₂ a b) ↔
      (a,b) ∈ Part.map Nat.unpair (eval p (Nat.pair l x)) :=
  left_tau ▸ endpoint_graph k p l x a b

theorem right_graph (k p l x a b : ℕ) :
    Graph (evaluatedTau .right) (pairPoint (point₂ k p) (point₂ l x)) (point₂ a b) ↔
      (a,b) ∈ Part.map Nat.unpair (eval p (Nat.pair l x)) :=
  right_tau ▸ endpoint_graph k p l x a b

theorem center_tuple_graph (k p₀ p₁ p₂ l x₀ x₁ x₂ h y₀ y₁ y₂ : ℕ) :
    Graph (evaluatedTau .zero) (pairPoint (point₄ k p₀ p₁ p₂) (point₄ l x₀ x₁ x₂)) (point₄ h y₀ y₁ y₂) ↔
      EffectiveFork.quadCode (h,y₀,y₁,y₂) ∈ eval p₀ (EffectiveFork.quadCode (l,x₀,x₁,x₂)) ∧
      Nat.pair h y₁ ∈ eval p₁ (Nat.pair l x₁) ∧ Nat.pair h y₂ ∈ eval p₂ (Nat.pair l x₂) := by

Proposition C.1 prop:missing-lift
Oracle-domain source C_K has recursion/range structure and faithful F; source diagonal separated, required conditional absent, disjoint arrows have no common upper bound.

CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.LocalConnectedness.OracleComputation.Restricted.missing_lift Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/OracleMissingLift.lean:243
theorem missing_lift :
    (∃ c c' : Idem (N K),Complementary c c' ∧ pair0 K ≤ c ∧ pair1 K ≤ c') ∧
    (¬ ∃ l : N K ⟶ coprod (N K) (N K),(inclusion K).map l=missingSection) ∧
    (∃ f g : N K ⟶ coprod (N K) (N K),
      Idem.ofRestrict f ⊓ Idem.ofRestrict g=⊥ ∧
        ¬ ∃ l,ArrowLE f l ∧ ArrowLE g l) :=
  ⟨⟨separator,separatorComplement,separator_complementary,
    pair0_le_separator,pair1_le_separatorComplement⟩,
    missingSection_has_no_lift,
    ⟨branchA,branchComplement,branch_restrictions_disjoint,branches_have_no_common_upper_bound⟩⟩


CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness.LocalConnectedness.OracleComputation.Restricted.no_lift_of_summand_inverse_images Lean/CategoricalEssentialIncompleteness/V46OracleSeparatorLift.lean:52
theorem no_lift_of_summand_inverse_images
    (s : PartialFun.of ℕ ⟶ coprod (PartialFun.of ℕ) (PartialFun.of ℕ))
    (h₀ : Idem.pull s (summand0 (PartialFun.of ℕ) (PartialFun.of ℕ)) =
      (rangeFunctor K).mapIdem separatorComplement)
    (h₁ : Idem.pull s (summand1 (PartialFun.of ℕ) (PartialFun.of ℕ)) =
      (rangeFunctor K).mapIdem separator) :
    ¬ ∃ ℓ : N K ⟶ coprod (N K) (N K),
      (inclusion K).map ℓ = s ≫ ((rangeFunctor K).comparison (N K) (N K)).hom :=
  no_lift_of_compatible_restriction (inducedRestriction_eq K) (rangeFunctor K)
    (tau K) (tau_isTuring K) (parityIso K) (inclusion_nonzero K) _ _
    ((rangeFunctor K).map_complementary separator_complementary)
    ((rangeFunctor K).map_mono pair0_le_separator)
    ((rangeFunctor K).map_mono pair1_le_separatorComplement) s h₀ h₁
